## Play, chat, Tactics/puzzles, Openings WLPP, Kids — what each computes, decides, remembers and says
These five surfaces compute a lot and connect almost none of it to one coach. Play computes threats, phase changes, tactic classifications and a planted gem slip on every move, then volunteers coaching as chat text through useCoachTips (default ON), which breaks S5. Meanwhile its live slip record is a no-op (raiseSlipPrompt returns because the hook is not interruptive). Play also grades moves with its own collapsed ladder and overrides it, so G7 fails. Chat runs three routers (an LLM reader, the fastPathLane shadow and the questionIntents regexes) feeding about 114 groundedAnswer assemblers that return bare strings. The in-game chat still post-validates model chess claims, and chat arrows are re-parsed from prose. Tactics keeps at least six independent puzzle-rating Elo writers (F16), has an LLM that grades Analysis Practice (F3), and never drills the gem library. Kid puzzle attempts also contaminate the adult theme skills. Openings: gems are no longer locked (areWeaponsUnlocked always returns true), and all 388 weapon-tier gems are narrated. But only 70 reach piece level, and the live callout fires only when the opponent actually plays the slip. In an ordinary line's Play rung the opponent plays the repertoire moves, and Play itself is silent. That is the concrete reason David has never heard a gem taught in a real game. For the one brain: wrong-try refutation, why-best-move, the hint and gem crush lines already carry their moves and squares and can fill TEMPTING-CHOICE and THE LINE directly. Nearly everything else emits a sentence with no role, no squares and no record path. components/Play/* is dead and can be deleted. Missing computers start with 'a pin breaks to a check', from David's own first gem.
MISSING: PIN BROKEN BY A CHECK (the brief's first gem): a pin only holds while the pinned piece cannot move with check, so a check breaks the pin and the pinning piece hangs. isRealPin (pinGeometry.ts:135-165) checks value, line-leaving, recapture and a hanging front piece, never 'the pinned piece moves with check'. Example: 1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 d6 3.Bc4 Bg4? 4.Bxf7+ Kxf7 5.Ng5+ Ke8 6.Qxg4 (and Légal's mate). Frequency: common in open games where Bg4 pins Nf3 (an estimate, not measured). Verb: recognize/consequence. Dual use: teach the shot, and catch the student pinning when the pin can be broken.; TRAP-CONDITION CHECKER (F04 'sound in some positions' patterns): for Bxf7+/Ng5+/Bxh7+/Qxg4 shapes, state which condition holds or fails ('does their queen cover g5? is g4 guarded? can the knight come back to f6?'), engine-verified. Only moveInsight.greekGift (Bxh7+) exists. Example: the same Bxf7+ with …Be7 able to answer Ng5+ vs with it not. Frequency: every recurring opening sacrifice. Verb: identify/consequence (also S12).; WRONG-COLOURED BISHOP + ROOK PAWN DRAW: grep finds no computer (wrongBishop/wrongRookPawn: 0 hits). Example: K+B(dark)+h-pawn vs K on h8 — a draw although a piece and a pawn up. Rare (endgames). Verb: knowledge.; RÉTI KING DIAGONAL / TWO GOALS AT ONCE: a king on the diagonal is as fast as a straight line, so it can chase two targets. No computer found (no Chebyshev/two-goals read; kingCourse in endgamePawnReads.ts:166 gives a king target only). Example: the 1921 Réti study, K on h8 catching the h-pawn while supporting its own c-pawn. Rare. Verb: knowledge (an F01 named nugget).; CONNECTED / PROTECTED PASSED PAWNS as a strategic asset: no computer (grep connectedPass/protected passer: 0); only conceptEngine's 'pawns-defend-each-other' matchup text. Example: pawns on e5+d6 protect each other — two connected passers on the sixth beat a rook. Occasional. Verb: strategize.; OPENING DEVELOPMENT/TEMPO COUNT: 'you have moved this piece twice / you are three moves behind in development / your queen came out early and will be chased'. No tempo-deficit computer found (grep tempoLoss/wasted tempo: none outside endgame spareTempoWasted; samePieceTwice in one file). Example: 1.e4 e5 2.Qh5 Nc6 3.Bc4 g6 4.Qf3 — the queen moved twice. Very common at club level (estimate). Verb: plan/prevent (F02 priority).; KING HUNT: driving an exposed king out with a chain of checks toward mate. No kingHunt/kingWalk computer. Example: Ed. Lasker–Thomas 1912 (Qxh7+!! and the king is walked to g1). Rare. Verb: recognize/consequence.; GOOD KNIGHT vs BAD BISHOP matchup verdict: zero hits. Example: a knight on d5 against a bishop on e7 hemmed in by its own pawns on d6/e5. Occasional in middlegames. Verb: strategize.; WINDMILL as a computed pattern: only text-table hits (groundedAnswer, voiceContainment). Example: Torre–Lasker 1925. Rare. Verb: recognize.; PUNISH-FOUND RECORD for planted or live gems (a capability, not a fact): nothing records whether the student found the punish after a gem slip in Play (CoachGamePage:2467) or OpeningPlayMode (findLivePunishment). F05 and F03 need it to know when traps stop deciding games. Every game in which a slip is planted. Verb: recognize (record side).; COUNT-BEFORE-YOU-CAPTURE as a live habit on Play and Tactics: the counting computer exists only as chat answers (chatTurnAnswers.answerCount :65) and pressureCount. Nothing applies it to the student's own pending capture to say 'you take, they recapture twice, you lose the exchange'. This is a wiring gap more than a new computer. Common. Verb: knowledge/consequence.
BROKEN: Play volunteers coaching as TEXT: useCoachTips is on by default (useSettings.ts:96/141, CoachGamePage.tsx:1471-1483). It injects 'Your opponent just made a serious error', tactic alerts, missed-tactic takeback offers and positional tips into chat, a bubble and arrows (handleCoachTip :1450). S5 breach; the playSilent gate (CoachGamePage.playSilent.test.ts:51) only scans voice speak sites. The hint nudge also injects chat text (CoachGamePage.tsx:1063-1068).; Play slip record is a no-op live: raiseSlipPrompt returns at useDiscussionPractice.ts:757 because Play never passes interruptive (:337). The comments at CoachGamePage.tsx:258/277 claiming 'the record is still filed' are false; only post-game autoAnalyzeGame records the slip.; useHintSystem records every hint as 'did not play the best move' (finalizeHintRequest playedMoveUci:null, useHintSystem.ts:226-231); legacy hint tiers 1/2 are dead (:243-258).; useCoachTips missed-tactic outcome goes only to an in-memory array (tacticAlertService.ts:628) and is never persisted (F5). isTacticWeakness is always false because computeTacticalProfile has no callers (tacticAlertService.ts:598, tacticalProfileService.ts:43).; Dead in Play: generateMoveCommentary (USE_LLM_MOVE_COMMENTARY=false, CoachGamePage:106/3511) and with it Play's explorer fetch and scanPositionForTrap (:3567-3600); useLiveCoach/liveCoachTriggers (enabled:false, only caller :1044); BLUNDER_CARD (:264); buildPlayEntryNarration (:660); the coach-turn tactic alert (:2962) and phase narration (:1923) are computed every move and suppressed.; Play's grade: classifyMoveFull folds best/excellent/good into 'good', and CoachGamePage then overrides the grade when the hint matched or analysis is missing (~:3305-3318). Review grades the same move again in gameAnalysisService (G4/G7/G8).; Play's planted gem slip (pickTaughtSlip, CoachGamePage:2467) is gated by rating (coachGameEngine.ts:239, F10/F03), never taught (Play is silent), and nothing records whether the student punished it.; Gem callout speaks percentages ('N% of club players play this move here', gemCrushLines.ts:786, V8) and uses three stock stems with no reason (:724-728). OpeningPlayMode records nothing about gem hold or miss.; Gems cannot fire in an ordinary Openings line/variation Play rung during the opening, because the opponent plays the repertoire moves (OpeningPlayMode.tsx:671-680). Only the gem-play rung feeds the inaccuracy. Tactics never drills the gem library.; Analysis Practice: the LLM decides correct/partial/wrong (positionReadingGrader.ts:64), which breaks F3.; In-game chat still validates model chess claims after the fact (GameChatPanel.tsx:1220-1245), which breaks F7/F3.; Kid puzzle attempts write the shared db.puzzles with the adult puzzleRating (KidPiecePuzzlesPage.tsx:120 → puzzleService.ts:709-740) and contaminate adult theme skills (getThemeSkills :324 → rankThemeTargets/getWeakestThemes/TacticalProfilePage). TacticCreatePage, CalculationTab and FindSquarePage write nothing to the student record (F5).; components/Play/* (GamesPage, GuessTheMove, OpeningChallenge, OpeningSpeedrun, BlindfolTrainer, GameCompleteCard) and services/gamesService.ts are unreachable: GamesPage is imported nowhere.; S10: chat (coachMoveExtractor.ts:55, GameChatPanel:1199), puzzles (arrowEngine.spokenLineArrows via PuzzleBoard:662) and lessons/gems (narrationArrows:180, mentionedMoveArrows:35) re-derive arrows from prose, even where the fact already carries squares (readWrongTry replyFrom/To and line).; COMPUTER_ROLES (computerRoles.ts) is keyed by Learn lanes only; Play, chat, Tactics, Openings and Kids have no dual-use parity table.; MiddlegamePractice speaks through speechService directly (MiddlegamePractice.tsx:303-364), bypassing the voiceService verbosity and audit-mute path.; Kid text allows square names (kidGameCoach.ts:57-60) — check against S11's 'no chess notation'.

## The student record: every writer and reader, heat map, capabilityEvidence, weakness spine, vocabularies, rating sources
The record has a real core: capabilityEvidence (one recorder, both directions, a measured green bar) and the weakness spine (nine sources folded into ranked holes with game provenance). Heat map, need score and moment boost read them. But it is not ONE record (R1/R4). (1) Six green rules disagree, and four lower a weakness by ABSENCE (lifecycle 'fixed', 'improving' trend, curriculum/dossier), against R3. (2) The red and green halves barely join: every analysis hole (game mistakes, tactics, puzzle misses, openings) has capabilityTag null. So a missed fork never turns a tile red, all tactics share one 'missed-tactic' tag, and the heat map's Practice button is dead. (3) Played and imported games record nothing positive until the student opens Review. Even then they record only 'held', never 'broken'. Traps are recorded only in Learn. (4) The vocabulary is split: 8 theme maps with a broken round trip and 3 spellings of 'removing the guard'. (5) Strength breaks F10/F11 and trap 1. The saved rating is a result-based Elo with 7 writers, including typed Elo and a first-run picker. The position-based liveStrength is thrown away after each game. The rating also gates book-departure need. (6) The LLM writes free-text student notes. For the one brain, the record should supply the REASON and HABIT roles ("you've walked into this fork in 3 games") and the praise. That needs one reader, per-motif and per-opening capability, rows that carry the posed squares/moves for arrows, and evidence recorded from every surface.
MISSING: PER-MOTIF CAPABILITY (fork, pin, skewer, discovered attack, back rank, deflection, overload, removing the guard, zwischenzug, x-ray, trapped piece): held/broken per motif so the heat map can say 'green on pins, red on forks'. Today all collapse to missed-tactic. Every game with a tactic; serves RECOGNIZE.; PER-OPENING / PER-LINE STRENGTH (F17): cpLoss against the engine at decision moments, grouped by opening family and variation, so 'strong in the Italian, weak against the Caro' is measured from moves, not from result share (resultDeficitTerm needScore.ts:427 uses results). Every game; KNOWLEDGE/STRATEGIZE.; PER-PHASE STRENGTH: opening vs middlegame vs endgame accuracy at posed questions, persisted (lifecycle only clusters mistakes by phase). Every game; STRATEGIZE.; PERSISTED POSITION-BASED STRENGTH: carry liveStrength across games as the one estimator (replacing the result Elo). Every game; other (opponent strength).; PER-TRAP / PER-GEM RECORD FROM ALL SURFACES: gem id walked into, punished, missed-punish, avoided — from Play, imported games and Review, not only Learn. Early-game, frequent at <1500; PREVENT/RECOGNIZE.; GEM PUNISHED UNPROMPTED = held evidence: the student found the refutation of an opponent slip without being told (gem lane held is 'na' computerRoles.ts:47). PRE-1500 frequent; RECOGNIZE/CONSEQUENCE.; F03 PROGRESSION METER: share of the student's decisive games decided by a trap/gem in the first ~15 moves, and its trend, to shift weight from traps to thinking. Per game; STRATEGIZE.; TERM-UNDERSTANDING MEMORY (V18): per student, which chess terms (pin, outpost, fianchetto, opposition) have been explained and whether they have since shown they understand them. Every session; KNOWLEDGE.; 'DODGED YOUR USUAL MISTAKE' detector (V6): a posed tag that is RED in the record answered cleanly now -> praise event mid-game (useProvenWatcher only fires on crossing green). Several times per game for a student with holes; RECOGNIZE (praise).; TAUGHT-CONCEPT LOG + IDEA-LEVEL SPACED REPETITION (brief 3d): what the coach explained, when, at which position, so the idea is re-tested later (teachingEffect only knows slips, SRS covers moves/puzzles). Every lesson; KNOWLEDGE.; CAPTURE-ARITHMETIC HABIT EVIDENCE: the student counted attackers vs defenders and declined a losing capture (or took a winning one) — held evidence for hung-material / bad-trade-material above the green bar. Most games; CONSEQUENCE/KNOWLEDGE.; CALCULATION DEPTH ESTIMATE: how many plies deep the student calculates correctly (calculation-depth is only a yes/no tag). Per tactical moment; CONSEQUENCE.; DECISION-TIME USE: clock spent at critical moments vs quiet ones (impulsive at forks in the road, slow on routine moves) — detectTimeTrouble covers only time trouble. Every timed game; PLAN (habit).; DEFENSIVE RESOURCEFULNESS (positive): held a worse or lost position to a draw / swindled — the mirror of botched-conversion. Maybe 1 game in 10; PREVENT.; NAMED ENDGAME TECHNIQUE PROOF: Lucena, Philidor, opposition, square of the pawn, triangulation, KBN mate — known (lesson) vs used (game) per technique. Rare in games (1 in 50-200), common in lessons; KNOWLEDGE.; OPPONENT-POPULATION RECORD: the slips the student's own opponents keep making in the student's openings (to pick which traps to teach). Per opening; PREVENT/PLAN.; PREVENTION EVIDENCE PER THREAT TYPE: stopped the opponent's plan or threat before it landed (prophylaxis maps only to the generic missed-opponents-threat). Most games; PREVENT.; PROVENANCE OF HELD EVIDENCE: bestSan + the squares/moves of the question posed on each capability row, so praise and 'you broke it again' can be arrowed (S10). Every row; other.
BROKEN: HEAT MAP 'Practice your positions' is dead: HeatMapPanel.ownPositionsHole (components/Insights/HeatMapPanel.tsx:81-85) needs an 'analysis:' hole whose capabilityTag equals the tile tag, but every analysis row sets capabilityTag: null (weaknessSpine.ts:394, :582, :622, :667, :693, :782, :804, :845), so selectedHole (:207) is always null and Practice is never offered.; Tactic motifs never reach the heat map: game mistakes, classified tactics and puzzle misses all carry capabilityTag null (weaknessSpine.ts:394, :622, :667), so a missed fork/pin from a game cannot turn any tile red. Only coach-counted misconception rows (:515) and fundamental rows (:476) can. And all motifs collapse into one tag 'missed-tactic' (puzzleMissService.ts:81, computerRoles.ts:47-48) — no per-motif green/red (violates F17/F9).; Absence lowers the record (violates R3): weaknessSignal.boostFor returns 0 for lifecycleStatus 'fixed' (weaknessSignal.ts:150) and subtracts 4 for trend 'improving' (:163); 'fixed' = no slips in the recent half of the timeline (weaknessLifecycle.ts:116). Same in studentMomentBoost.holeOpensMoment (studentMomentBoost.ts:108), teachingLayers.holeIsOpen (teachingLayers.ts:86), rankThemeTargets (puzzleThemeTargets.ts:53), studentDossier strengths (studentDossier.ts:97-102), coachCurriculumService mastered (:91). The comment at weaknessSignal.ts:153 claims 'proven' is the only lowering input — the line above it contradicts that.; Six green/fixed rules disagree (R4): capabilityProven (capabilityEvidence.ts:265), lifecycle 'fixed', trapDecision green after ONE unprompted avoid (trapLearning.ts:57), mistakePuzzle 'mastered' by SRS reps (mistakePuzzleService.ts:1388), curriculum 'mastered' (coachCurriculumService.ts:91), dossier strengths that never read capabilityProven (studentDossier.ts:97). rankThemeTargets ignores WeaknessSignal.proven (puzzleThemeTargets.ts:52-54).; Played and imported games write NO positive evidence and NO broken rows unless the student opens Review: positive half gated on opts.reviewed (autoAnalyzeGame.ts:423, :455, :474); Play finish calls without reviewed (CoachGamePage.tsx:2118), import sweep too (gameAnalysisService.ts:2289, autoAnalyzeGame.ts:662). Even when reviewed, capabilityPliesFromAnnotations skips blunder/mistake plies (autoAnalyzeGame.ts:284), so the game sweep only ever writes 'held', never 'broken' (F5 note).; PLAUSIBLE: 'hung-material' (the most common amateur hole) has no path to green from games: no MOVE_FUNDAMENTAL_TAG maps to it (moveFundamentals.ts:158-193) and moveInsight.positionPosed poses it only at importance 60 (moveInsight.ts:756, :760), below PROVEN_MIN_IMPORTANCE 80 (capabilityEvidence.ts:222); the blunderCheck lane records no held (computerRoles.ts:55). Same 60-70 ceiling for passive-rook, misplaced-piece, mistimed-pawn-break, weakened-king-safety, bad-trade from this source.; Tactic hole double-count: mergeByKey adds openCount/total (weaknessSpine.ts:868-869) for mistakePuzzles and classifiedTactics built from the same annotations (census finding 2).; Theme<->tactic round trip breaks: LICHESS_THEME_TO_TACTIC capturingDefender->removing_the_guard (tacticClassifierService.ts:56) but themesForTactic removing_the_guard->defensiveMove and overloaded_piece->capturingDefender (weaknessSpine.ts:352-353); a third spelling 'removal_of_guard' (danyaTeachingService.ts:1257); 8 maps total incl. puzzleGenerator.ts:120 and puzzleConceptExplanation.ts:43 (R5).; Dead reader: tacticalProfileService.computeTacticalProfile has 0 callers, so getStoredTacticalProfile is always null and tacticAlertService.isTacticWeakness (:598) always false in useCoachTips.ts:284/:323; getTacticMotifStats 0 callers.; recordPromptedFind always writes prompted:true (gameAnalysisService.ts:1090), so Review turning-point finds never move the heat map in either direction (by design, but it means Review answers never prove or break anything).; A rating decides what the coach says (violates F10): book-departure weakness thresholds read the rating (bookDepartureWeakness.ts:79-81) and feed needScore.departureTerm (needScore.ts:253) and the spine (weaknessSpine.ts:956-958, which falls back to puzzleRating — a different skill).; Strength is result-based, not position-based (CLAUDE.md trap 1, F11): the persisted rating is a K=32 ELO over coach-game RESULTS (playerRatingService.ts:80-117) rewritten into currentRating at boot (strengthCalibrationService.ts:205); the position-based liveStrength is reset per game (useDiscussionPractice.ts:357-359) and never saved, so every game reseeds from the result Elo — two estimators where the rule says one.; currentRating has 7 writers, not 5: OnboardingPage.tsx:52, SettingsPage.tsx:614-619 (typed Elo, self-reported — violates F11/F15), ImportPage.tsx:212, GameImportCard.tsx:135, gameAnalysisService.ts:2979 (profile-name substring match, can match the wrong player), strengthCalibrationService.ts:97/:205, dbService.ts:29 default. FirstRunStrength (App.tsx:758) + isBeginnerMode (ratingBands.ts:87) still exist though F15 drops them.; Rating defaults still disagree: 400 (ratingBands.ts:65), 800 puzzle (studentPuzzleRating.ts:31), 1200 endgame (adaptiveEndgameService.ts:73), 1200 coachActuator.ts:551, 1200 appStore.ts:292 and userContext.ts:33, 1200 mistakePuzzleService.ts:358, 100 kid (kidRatingService.ts:22).; LLM writes the student record: coachMemoryService stores free-text notes the model emits via [[REMEMBER:]] (coachMemoryService.ts:101, called coachAgentRunner.ts:258, VoiceChatMic.tsx:519) and injects them into every prompt (:85) — an uncomputed parallel record (G0).; teachingEffect computes 'is the taught mistake declining' and only logs it (teachingEffectService.ts:23-45); nothing praises it (V6) — computed and dropped.; Trap record exists only in Learn (noteTrapMeeting / trapSpeaks callers: learnBoardTeaching.ts:526, :574); a gem walked into in Play, Review or an imported game feeds only liveStrength (useDiscussionPractice.ts:374) and is never recorded per trap, so F02 'warned until they prove they avoid it' cannot see real games. Trap rows share tag missed-opponents-threat with prophylaxis/threat rows (trapLearning.ts:23) and are separated only by FEN.; capabilityEvidence rows carry fen + playedSan but no bestSan and no squares (capabilityEvidence.ts:139-180), so a 'you held it' or 'you broke it again' line cannot draw the arrow of what was held (S10).; Legacy weaknessAnalyzer profile still read on Play/Stats/Learn/chat (census B1), getMistakeInsights backs /weaknesses separately from the spine — 6 readers of 'is this a weakness'.
